Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the dynamics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 17 and the convergence rate is O(513/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by dynamics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 17, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 513. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The dynamics variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 223 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 119 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 223 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dynamics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 119. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
